# Physical reference profiles: complete catalog

Generated from ReptTraits v1-2 and curated species descriptions. Every catalog source label has an entry. Coverage differs by species; unknowns are explicit. The four detailed starter profiles remain authoritative companion documents.

**Interpretation:** Workbook lengths are reported maxima, never typical adult sizes. SVL excludes the tail. Juvenile values have a header/definition conflict and require review. Color categories are coarse compilation labels, not an exhaustive palette or a rendering preset. A missing pattern is not evidence of a patternless snake. Dataset values can differ from field accounts, including the starter profiles; neither is silently overwritten.

**Scope:** These are species references, not measurements or annotations of the pilot photographs. Descriptions and image review are independent. No phenotype is inferred from venom. Body/head ratios and absolute colors remain unset. Some references describe regional populations or historical species complexes. The original venom taxonomy is preserved.

Entries: 174; workbook matches: 170; with newly extracted descriptive traits: 149; starter references: 4.

[Data publication](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41597-024-03079-5) · [Dataset](https://doi.org/10.6084/m9.figshare.24572683) · [Starter profiles](../StarterSpeciesPhysicalProfiles.md)

For the engine, join `data/processed/morphology/physical_profiles.json` to the species catalog on `species_id`. Citations and workbook cell addresses are preserved per trait. The workbook bibliography is retained in the extraction snapshot. Compilation facts, supplementary descriptions and proposed rendering controls remain separate.

## Agkistrodon bilineatus

Catalog ID: `source_14e6e136019bef41`. Family in workbook: Viperidae. Profile coverage: descriptive reference.

Pilot folders: `data/image_pilots/pilot_010_agkistrodon_bilineatus`

### Size and compiled traits

- **maximum total length mm:** 1380. Source: ReptTraits `Data!AC357`; Feldman et al. 2016.
- **maximum svl mm:** 337. Source: ReptTraits `Data!AD357`; Moon & Candy 1997.
- **maximum female svl mm:** Not reported in the matched compilation record.
- **maximum male svl mm:** Not reported in the matched compilation record.
- **juvenile svl mm ambiguous statistic:** Not reported in the matched compilation record.
- **dorsal colour:** Black/Brown. Source: ReptTraits `Data!V357`; Snakedb.org 2023.
- **dorsal pattern:** Banded. Source: ReptTraits `Data!W357`; Snakedb.org 2023.
- **pupil shape:** Not reported in the matched compilation record.
- **fang position:** front-fanged. Source: ReptTraits `Data!Z357`; Snakedb.org 2023.

### Shape, surface and appearance

- **Body:** Thick trunk, short slender tail. [Source](https://sistemaestatalambiental.chiapas.gob.mx/siseiach/descargas/fraccion13/publicaciones/libros/05.pdf)
- **Head:** Triangular head with contrasting pale facial lines. [Source](https://sistemaestatalambiental.chiapas.gob.mx/siseiach/descargas/fraccion13/publicaciones/libros/05.pdf)
- **Tail:** No species-specific description extracted for this field; leave unknown.
- **Scales:** Strongly keeled. [Source](https://sistemaestatalambiental.chiapas.gob.mx/siseiach/descargas/fraccion13/publicaciones/libros/05.pdf)
- **Pattern:** Adults dark with pale dotted markings; juveniles have clearer crossbands. [Source](https://sistemaestatalambiental.chiapas.gob.mx/siseiach/descargas/fraccion13/publicaciones/libros/05.pdf)
- **Ventral:** No species-specific description extracted for this field; leave unknown.
- **Eyes:** No species-specific description extracted for this field; leave unknown.
- **Special:** No species-specific description extracted for this field; leave unknown.
- **Variation:** Young animals have a yellowish tail tip. [Source](https://sistemaestatalambiental.chiapas.gob.mx/siseiach/descargas/fraccion13/publicaciones/libros/05.pdf)

### Variation, limits and proposed rendering controls

- **Unmeasured:** head/body width ratios, tail/body length ratios, calibrated colors and population distributions.
- **Proposed controls:** `dorsal_palette`, `head_neck_transition`, `head_outline`, `life_stage_variant`, `locality_variant`, `pattern_contrast`, `pattern_geometry`, `sex_variant`, `surface_texture`, `trunk_taper`, `trunk_width`. These are interface/design suggestions, not biological measurements.
- **Reference views:** full-body dorsal/lateral, head detail, and ventral detail only when relevant. No image review is required to read this profile.

## Agkistrodon contortrix

Catalog ID: `source_6bc177d7002c458f`. Family in workbook: Viperidae. Profile coverage: descriptive reference.

Pilot folders: `data/image_pilots/pilot_011_agkistrodon_contortrix`

### Size and compiled traits

- **maximum total length mm:** 1350. Source: ReptTraits `Data!AC359`; Feldman et al. 2016.
- **maximum svl mm:** 760. Source: ReptTraits `Data!AD359`; Ford et al. 1990, Schuett & Gillingham 1989.
- **maximum female svl mm:** 660. Source: ReptTraits `Data!AE359`; Carey and Judge 2002.
- **maximum male svl mm:** 760. Source: ReptTraits `Data!AF359`; Carey and Judge 2002.
- **juvenile svl mm ambiguous statistic:** 200. Source: ReptTraits `Data!AG359`; Carey and Judge 2002. **Requires review.**
- **dorsal colour:** Brown/Gray/Red. Source: ReptTraits `Data!V359`; Snakedb.org 2023.
- **dorsal pattern:** Banded. Source: ReptTraits `Data!W359`; Snakedb.org 2023.
- **pupil shape:** Not reported in the matched compilation record.
- **fang position:** front-fanged. Source: ReptTraits `Data!Z359`; Snakedb.org 2023.

### Shape, surface and appearance

- **Body:** No species-specific description extracted for this field; leave unknown.
- **Head:** No species-specific description extracted for this field; leave unknown.
- **Tail:** No species-specific description extracted for this field; leave unknown.
- **Scales:** No species-specific description extracted for this field; leave unknown.
- **Pattern:** Brown crossbands on a lighter ground; head copper-colored. [Source](https://tpwd.texas.gov/huntwild/wild/species/southerncopperhead/)
- **Ventral:** No species-specific description extracted for this field; leave unknown.
- **Eyes:** No species-specific description extracted for this field; leave unknown.
- **Special:** No species-specific description extracted for this field; leave unknown.
- **Variation:** No species-specific description extracted for this field; leave unknown.

### Variation, limits and proposed rendering controls

- **Source scope:** Account uses the historical southern-copperhead subspecies name; retain geographic scope.
- **Data issue:** Juvenile column header says maximum but definition says mean; statistic unresolved.
- **Unmeasured:** head/body width ratios, tail/body length ratios, calibrated colors and population distributions.
- **Proposed controls:** `dorsal_palette`, `pattern_contrast`, `pattern_geometry`. These are interface/design suggestions, not biological measurements.
- **Reference views:** full-body dorsal/lateral, head detail, and ventral detail only when relevant. No image review is required to read this profile.

## Agkistrodon piscivorus

Catalog ID: `source_0d6a65964a227910`. Family in workbook: Viperidae. Profile coverage: descriptive reference.

Pilot folders: `data/image_pilots/pilot_012_agkistrodon_piscivorus`

### Size and compiled traits

- **maximum total length mm:** 1892. Source: ReptTraits `Data!AC362`; Seymor 1987.
- **maximum svl mm:** 580. Source: ReptTraits `Data!AD362`; Ford et al. 1990.
- **maximum female svl mm:** Not reported in the matched compilation record.
- **maximum male svl mm:** Not reported in the matched compilation record.
- **juvenile svl mm ambiguous statistic:** 350. Source: ReptTraits `Data!AG362`; Carey and Judge 2002. **Requires review.**
- **dorsal colour:** Black. Source: ReptTraits `Data!V362`; Snakedb.org 2023.
- **dorsal pattern:** Not reported in the matched compilation record.
- **pupil shape:** slit (or vertical elliptical). Source: ReptTraits `Data!Y362`; Snakedb.org 2023.
- **fang position:** front-fanged. Source: ReptTraits `Data!Z362`; Snakedb.org 2023.

### Shape, surface and appearance

- **Body:** Heavy-bodied. [Source](https://www.outdooralabama.com/venomous-snakes/cottonmouth)
- **Head:** No species-specific description extracted for this field; leave unknown.
- **Tail:** No species-specific description extracted for this field; leave unknown.
- **Scales:** No species-specific description extracted for this field; leave unknown.
- **Pattern:** Dark adults can obscure earlier crossbanding. [Source](https://www.outdooralabama.com/venomous-snakes/cottonmouth)
- **Ventral:** No species-specific description extracted for this field; leave unknown.
- **Eyes:** No species-specific description extracted for this field; leave unknown.
- **Special:** Pale mouth interior exposed during defensive gaping. [Source](https://www.outdooralabama.com/venomous-snakes/cottonmouth)
- **Variation:** No species-specific description extracted for this field; leave unknown.

### Variation, limits and proposed rendering controls

- **Source scope:** Historical range statements may include the separately recognized Florida cottonmouth; do not transfer its photographs automatically.
- **Data issue:** Juvenile column header says maximum but definition says mean; statistic unresolved.
- **Unmeasured:** head/body width ratios, tail/body length ratios, calibrated colors and population distributions.
- **Proposed controls:** `display_state`, `dorsal_palette`, `pattern_contrast`, `pattern_geometry`, `special_feature_geometry`, `trunk_taper`, `trunk_width`. These are interface/design suggestions, not biological measurements.
- **Reference views:** full-body dorsal/lateral, head detail, and ventral detail only when relevant. No image review is required to read this profile.

## Aipysurus laevis

Catalog ID: `source_1f2e6b93d5b4d25d`. Family in workbook: Elapidae. Profile coverage: descriptive reference.

### Shape, surface and appearance

See the existing [detailed starter description](../StarterSpeciesPhysicalProfiles.md) for this species. The compilation below/above is an additional source, not a replacement.

- **Body:** Heavy trunk and narrow neck. [Source](https://www.californiaherps.com/snakes/pages/c.atrox.html)
- **Head:** Broad triangular head. [Source](https://www.californiaherps.com/snakes/pages/c.atrox.html)
- **Tail:** Segmented rattle; newborn button. [Source](https://www.californiaherps.com/snakes/pages/c.atrox.html)
- **Scales:** Keeled. [Source](https://www.californiaherps.com/snakes/pages/c.atrox.html)
- **Pattern:** Pale-edged dorsal diamonds and contrasting tail rings. [Source](https://www.californiaherps.com/snakes/pages/c.atrox.html)
- **Ventral:** No species-specific description extracted for this field; leave unknown.
- **Eyes:** No species-specific description extracted for this field; leave unknown.
- **Special:** No species-specific description extracted for this field; leave unknown.
- **Variation:** Young animals often more distinctly patterned. [Source](https://www.californiaherps.com/snakes/pages/c.atrox.html)

- **Head:** Dark triangular head broader than neck. [Source](https://www.mdpi.com/2072-6651/13/8/514)
- **Tail:** No species-specific description extracted for this field; leave unknown.
- **Scales:** Small smooth head and body scales in the description. [Source](https://www.mdpi.com/2072-6651/13/8/514)
- **Pattern:** Brown or grey-brown with squarish dorsal blotches. [Source](https://www.mdpi.com/2072-6651/13/8/514)
- **Ventral:** No species-specific description extracted for this field; leave unknown.
- **Eyes:** No species-specific description extracted for this field; leave unknown.
- **Special:** No species-specific description extracted for this field; leave unknown.
- **Variation:** No species-specific description extracted for this field; leave unknown.

### Variation, limits and proposed rendering controls

- **Source scope:** The paper explicitly uses peninsular-Malaysian convictus as the morphological example; do not transfer it to every Ovophis.

- **Eyes:** Large orange-brown iris, round pupil. [Source](https://australian.museum/learn/animals/reptiles/coastal-taipan/)

- **Variation:** Age and season affect color. [Source](https://australian.museum/learn/animals/reptiles/coastal-taipan/)
